# Agents · R · part 25

1,098 entries, review-swarm-design-correctness to reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-swarm-design-correctness**: Read-only design-correctness reviewer used by the review-swarm skill. Finds logic bugs a static analyzer can't catch — off-by-one errors, wrong operators, bad control flow, mishandled edge cases. Ignores security and test quality. ([CodeWithSally/CodeWithSally-Apex-English](https://github.com/CodeWithSally/CodeWithSally-Apex-English/blob/HEAD/Sessions/ClaudeCode/Session006/.claude/agents/review-swarm-design-correctness.md) · ★15)
- **review-swarm-security**: Read-only security reviewer used by the review-swarm skill. Finds exploitable security holes — injection, auth/authz gaps, secrets, unsafe deserialization, SSRF, path traversal. Ignores test coverage and general logic bugs. ([CodeWithSally/CodeWithSally-Apex-English](https://github.com/CodeWithSally/CodeWithSally-Apex-English/blob/HEAD/Sessions/ClaudeCode/Session006/.claude/agents/review-swarm-security.md) · ★15)
- **review-swarm-test-quality**: Read-only test-quality reviewer used by the review-swarm skill. Checks whether tests actually assert meaningful behavior — flags weak/tautological assertions, over-mocking, and missing edge-case coverage. Ignores security and general logic bugs. ([CodeWithSally/CodeWithSally-Apex-English](https://github.com/CodeWithSally/CodeWithSally-Apex-English/blob/HEAD/Sessions/ClaudeCode/Session006/.claude/agents/review-swarm-test-quality.md) · ★15)
